# THE THIRD BACKPORT SET — live-edited stock images (6248 rows).
#
# protect-list/ is a PRESENCE diff: it finds paths the live client has and the stock trees
# do not. It is structurally blind to a path that exists in BOTH trees and whose CONTENT the
# owner changed. Under a v84 base those images arrive from v84 and his edits vanish silently.
# No merge conflict is raised, because there is no manifest row to conflict with.
#
# Source: modified-list/<X>.live.txt (v83-stock vs live client, WzImage.BlockSize differs),
# intersected with modified-list/<X>.txt (v83-stock vs v84) to classify each row.
#
#   LIVE-ONLY (6112) — v84 shipped this image unchanged from v83. The live image can be
#                      restored WHOLESALE over the v84 one. Mechanical, one op per image.
#   CONFLICT  (136)  — v84 ALSO edited this image. Three-way; needs a human decision per row.
#
# Caveat, stated: BlockSize is a change DETECTOR, not a content hash (TOOL-NOTES.md).
# 24 of the 6248 differ by <=4 bytes and are probably re-encode noise; a same-length
# replacement would be invisible entirely. Escalate to WzMerge hash per image if that matters.
#
# Columns: path, v83 bytes, live bytes, class.
